## 📝 Duplicate Page Titles

Pages should have a descriptive, unique `<title>` so users can tell them apart in browser tabs, history, and bookmarks ([WCAG 2.4.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/page-titled.html)).

- **"Digital Service at CMS | CMS"** — 2 pages:
  - https://www.cms.gov/digital-service
  - https://www.cms.gov/about-cms/who-we-are/leadership/digital-service-cms
- **"Error Rate Findings and Results | CMS"** — 2 pages:
  - https://www.cms.gov/data-research/monitoring-programs/improper-payment-measurement-programs/medicare-part-c-ipm/error-rate-findings-results
  - https://www.cms.gov/data-research/monitoring-programs/improper-payment-measurement-programs/medicare-part-d-ipm/error-rate-findings-results

## 🔧 Most Common Issues (ALFA)

These ALFA accessibility issues appear most frequently across your pages:

| Rule | Pages Affected | Documentation |
|---|---:|---|
| [SIA-R111](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111): Interactive elements have a sufficient target size | **61** of 108 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111) |
| [SIA-R61](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r61): The first heading in the document is a level 1 heading | **61** of 108 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r61) |
| [SIA-R73](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73): Paragraphs of text have a line height that is not too small | **61** of 108 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73) |
| [SIA-R1](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r1): The document has a non-empty <title> element | **26** of 108 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r1) |
| [SIA-R4](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r4): The html element has a non-empty lang attribute | **26** of 108 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r4) |
| [SIA-R59](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r59): Document has at least one heading to identify its main content | **26** of 108 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r59) |
| [SIA-R87](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r87): The first focusable element on the page is a skip link to the main content | **26** of 108 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r87) |

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

#### Pattern 1: Affects 61 page(s) - 61 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**Issue**: Target has insufficient size

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<a href="/about-cms" class="menu-item-link" data-drupal-link-system-path="node/132926" data-once="navigationLinkMatch">About CMS</a>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/a[@class="menu-item-link"]
```
